Build a **kanban board** in a single self-contained HTML file. Clean, modern


column layout; cards should feel like cards. The graded contract:





**Board**


- Three columns with ids `#col-todo`, `#col-doing`, `#col-done`, each with a


  visible header and a card container.


- Each column header shows a live count of its cards in an element with class


  `.count` inside that column.





**Cards**


- `#new-card` — a text input; `#add-card` — a button that creates a card in


  **To Do** from the input's text (and clears the input). Empty input adds


  nothing.


- Every card is an element with class `.card` whose visible text includes its


  title, plus a delete button with class `.card-del` (deletes immediately —


  no confirm dialog).


- Cards are **draggable between columns with HTML5 drag-and-drop**


  (`draggable="true"`, proper `dragstart`/`dragover`/`drop` handlers on the


  columns). Dropping a card on a column moves it there and updates both


  counts.





**Persistence**


- The full board state (cards + which column each is in) persists in


  `localStorage`: a page reload restores every card to the right column.





Single file, all CSS/JS inline, no external resources. Every control must


actually work — no dead UI, no stub functions.

(function() {


  var STORAGE_KEY = 'kanban-board';





  function generateId() {


    return 'card-' + Date.now().toString(36) + '-' + Math.random().toString(36).substr(2, 9);


  }





  function loadState() {


    try {


      var raw = localStorage.getItem(STORAGE_KEY);


      if (raw) {


        var data = JSON.parse(raw);


        if (data && Array.isArray(data.cards)) {


          return data;


        }


      }


    } catch (e) {}


    return { cards: [] };


  }





  function saveState(state) {


    try {


      localStorage.setItem(STORAGE_KEY, JSON.stringify(state));


    } catch (e) {}


  }





  function getColumnKey(columnId) {


    return columnId.replace('col-', '');


  }





  function render(state) {


    var columns = {


      todo: document.getElementById('col-todo'),


      doing: document.getElementById('col-doing'),


      done: document.getElementById('col-done')


    };


    Object.values(columns).forEach(function(col) {


      var container = col.querySelector('.card-container');


      if (container) container.innerHTML = '';


    });





    state.cards.forEach(function(card) {


      var col = columns[card.column];


      if (!col) return;


      var container = col.querySelector('.card-container');


      var el = document.createElement('div');


      el.className = 'card';


      el.draggable = true;


      el.dataset.id = card.id;





      var title = document.createElement('span');


      title.className = 'card-title';


      title.textContent = card.title;


      el.appendChild(title);

```html


<!DOCTYPE html>


<html lang="en">


<head>


<meta charset="UTF-8">


<title>Kanban Board</title>


<style>


  body{font-family:Arial,sans-serif;background:#f0f2f5;margin:0;padding:20px;}


  .board{display:flex;gap:20px;justify-content:center;}


  .column{background:#fff;border-radius:6px;width:300px;box-shadow:0 2px 4px rgba(0,0,0,.1);display:flex;flex-direction:column;}


  .column h2{margin:0;padding:10px;background:#f7f8fa;border-bottom:1px solid #e0e0e0;font-size:16px;color:#333;display:flex;justify-content:space-between;align-items:center;}


  .card-container{flex-grow:1;padding:10px;min-height:100px;display:flex;flex-direction:column;gap:8px;}


  .card{background:#fafafa;border-radius:4px;padding:8px 12px;box-shadow:0 1px 2px rgba(0,0,0,.05);position:relative;cursor:grab;}


  .card:hover{box-shadow:0 2px 4px rgba(0,0,0,.1);}


  .card-del{position:absolute;top:4px;right:4px;background:#e74c3c;color:#fff;border:none;border-radius:50%;width:18px;height:18px;font-size:12px;cursor:pointer;}


  .add-section{margin-bottom:20px;text-align:center;}


  #new-card{padding:8px;width:200px;max-width:80%;border:1px solid #ccc;border-radius:4px;}


  #add-card{padding:8px 12px;margin-left:6px;background:#27ae60;color:white;border:none;border-radius:4px;cursor:pointer;}


</style>


</head>


<body>





<div class="add-section">


  <input type="text" id="new-card" placeholder="New task...">


  <button id="add-card">Add Card</button>


</div>





<div class="board">


  <div id="col-todo" class="column" data-col="todo">


    <h2>To Do (<span class="count">0</span>)</h2>


    <div class="card-container"></div>


  </div>


  <div id="col-doing" class="column" data-col="doing">


    <h2>Doing (<span class="count">0</span>)</h2>


    <div class="card-container"></div>


  </div>


  <div id="col-done" class="column" data-col="done">


    <h2>Done (<span class="count">0</span>)</h2>


    <div class="card-container"></div>


  </div>


</div>





<script>


(() => {


  const STORAGE_KEY = 'kanban-state';


  let state = []; // [{id, title, column}]


  


  function saveState() { localStorage.setItem(STORAGE_KEY, JSON.stringify(state)); }


  function loadState() { 


    const data = localStorage.getItem(STORAGE_KEY);


    if (data) state = JSON.parse(data);


  }


  


  function createCardElement(card){ 


    const el = document.createElement('div');


    el.className='card';


    el.setAttribute('draggable','true');


    el.dataset.id=card.id;


    el.textContent=card.title;


    


    const delBtn=document.createElement('button');


    delBtn.className='card-del';


    delBtn.innerHTML='×';


    delBtn.onclick=(e)=>{


      e.stopPropagation();


      deleteCard(card.id);


    };


    el.appendChild(delBtn);


    


    el.addEventListener('dragstart', (e)=>{ e.dataTransfer.setData('text/plain', card.id); });


    return el;


  }


  


  function renderBoard(){ 


    const cols=document.querySelectorAll('.column');


    cols.forEach(col=>{


      const container=col.querySelector('.card-container');


      container.innerHTML='';


      state.filter(c=>c.column===col.dataset.col).forEach(card=>{


        container.appendChild(createCardElement(card));


      });


      col.querySelector('.count').textContent = state.filter(c=>c.column===col.dataset.col).length;


    });


  }


  


  function addCard(title){ 


    if (!title.trim()) return;


    const id=Date.now().toString();


    state.push({id, title:title.trim(), column:'todo'});


    saveState(); renderBoard();


  }


  


  function deleteCard(id){


    state = state.filter(c=>c.id!==id);


    saveState(); renderBoard();


  }


  


  function moveCard(id, targetCol){ 


    const card=state.find(c=>c.id===id);


    if (!card) return;


    card.column=targetCol;


    saveState(); renderBoard();


  }


  


  // Drag & Drop


  document.querySelectorAll('.column').forEach(col=>{


    col.addEventListener('dragover', e=>{ e.preventDefault(); });


    col.addEventListener('drop', e=>{


      e.preventDefault();


      const id=e.dataTransfer.getData('text/plain');


      moveCard(id, col.dataset.col);


    });


  });


  


  // Add button


  document.getElementById('add-card').onclick=()=>{


    const input=document.getElementById('new-card');


    addCard(input.value);


    input.value='';


  };


  


  // Init


  loadState(); renderBoard();


})();


</script>


</body>


</html>


```

<script>


const STORAGE_KEY='kanban-board-v1';


let state={todo:[],doing:[],done:[]};





function save(){localStorage.setItem(STORAGE_KEY,JSON.stringify(state));}


function load(){


  try{const raw=localStorage.getItem(STORAGE_KEY); if(raw){state=JSON.parse(raw);}}catch(e){}


  if(!state.todo) state.todo=[];


  if(!state.doing) state.doing=[];


  if(!state.done) state.done=[];


}


function uid(){return Date.now().toString(36)+Math.random().toString(36).slice(2);}


function findCard(id){


  for(const col of ['todo','doing','done']){


    const idx=state[col].findIndex(c=>c.id===id);


    if(idx>-1) return {col,idx,card:state[col][idx]};


  }


  return null;


}


function updateCounts(){


  for(const col of ['todo','doing','done']){


    const el=document.querySelector('#col-'+col+' .count');


    if(el) el.textContent=state[col].length;


  }


}


function makeCard(card){


  const el=document.createElement('div');


  el.className='card';


  el.draggable=true;


  el.dataset.id=card.id;


  const title=document.createElement('span');


  title.className='title';


  title.textContent=card.title;


  const del=document.createElement('button');


  del.className='card-del';


  del.textContent='×';


  del.setAttribute('aria-label','Delete');


  el.appendChild(title);


  el.appendChild(del);


  el.addEventListener('dragstart',e=>{


    e.dataTransfer.setData('text/plain',card.id);


    e.dataTransfer.effectAllowed='move';


  });


  del.addEventListener('click',e=>{


    e.stopPropagation();


    deleteCard(el);


  });


  return el;


}


function deleteCard(el){


  const id=el.dataset.id;


  const found=findCard(id);


  if(!found) return;


  state[found.col].splice(found.idx,1);


  el.remove();


  save(); updateCounts();


}


function render(){


  for(const col of ['todo','doing','done']){


    const container=document.querySelector('#col-'+col+' .cards');


    container.innerHTML='';


    for(const card of state[col]){


      container.appendChild(makeCard(card));


    }


  }


  updateCounts();


}


function addCard(title){


  const t=title.trim();


  if(!t) return;


  const card={id:uid(),title:t};


  state.todo.push(card);


  const container=document.querySelector('#col-todo .cards');


  container.appendChild(makeCard(card));


  save(); updateCounts();


  document.getElementById('new-card').value='';


}


document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded',()=>{


  load();


  render();


  document.getElementById('add-card').addEventListener('click',()=>addCard(document.getElementById('new-card').value));


  document.getElementById('new-card').addEventListener('keydown',e=>{if(e.key==='Enter') addCard(e.target.value);});


  for(const col of ['todo','doing','done']){


    const column=document.getElementById('col-'+col);


    column.addEventListener('dragover',e=>{e.preventDefault()


…[truncated, 783 more chars in transcript]

    </div>

  <script>


    let draggedCard = null;





    function createCard(text) {


      const card = document.createElement('div');


      card.className = 'card';


      card.setAttribute('draggable', true);


      card.innerHTML = `<span class="card-text">${text}</span><button class="card-del">✖</button>`;


      


      card.addEventListener('dragstart', (e) => {


        draggedCard = e.target;


      });





      card.querySelector('.card-del').addEventListener('click', () => {


        card.remove();


        updateCounts();


        saveState();


      });





      return card;


    }





    function updateCounts() {


      document.querySelectorAll('.column').forEach(col => {


        const countEl = col.querySelector('.count');


        const cards = col.querySelector('.card-container').children.length;


        countEl.textContent = cards;


      });


    }





    function saveState() {


      const state = { 


        cards: Array.from(document.querySelectorAll('.card')).map(card => ({


          text: card.querySelector('.card-text')?.textContent.trim(),


          columnId: card.closest('.column').id


        }))


      };


      localStorage.setItem('kanban-state', JSON.stringify(state));


    }





    document.getElementById('add-card').addEventListener('click', () => {


      const input = document.getElementById('new-card');


      const text = input.value.trim();


      if (text) {


        const card = createCard(text);


        document.querySelector('#col-todo .card-container').appendChild(card);


        input.value = '';


        updateCounts();


        saveState();


      }


    });





    document.getElementById('new-card').addEventListener('keypress', e => {


      if (e.key === 'Enter') document.getElementById('add-card').click();


    });





    function handleDragOver(e) {


      e.preventDefault();


      this.classList.add('dragover');


    }





    function handleDrop() {


      if (!draggedCard || draggedCard === this) return;


      this.classList.remove('dragover');


      this.appendChild(draggedCard);


      updateCounts();


      saveState();


    }





    document.querySelectorAll('.card-container').forEach(container => {


      container.addEventListener('dragover', handleDragOver);


      container.addEventListener('drop', handleDrop);


    });





    // Load state


    const saved = localStorage.getItem('kanban-state');


    if (saved) {


      const { cards } = JSON.parse(saved);


      cards.forEach(data => {


        if (!data.text) return;


        const card = createCard(data.text);


        document.getElementById(data.columnId)?.querySelector('.card-container')?.appendChild(card);


      });


    }


    updateCounts();


  </script>


</body>


</html>

<script>


  // Load board state from localStorage


  function loadBoard() {


    const cols = document.querySelectorAll('.column');


    const savedData = localStorage.getItem('kanbanData');


    if (savedData) {


      const data = JSON.parse(savedData);


      cols.forEach(col => {


        const container = col.querySelector('.card-container');


        container.innerHTML = '';


        const colId = col.id;


        if (data[colId]) {


          data[colId].forEach(cardText => {


            const card = createCardElement(cardText);


            container.appendChild(card);


          });


        }


        updateCount(col);


      });


    }


  }





  // Create a card element


  function createCardElement(text) {


    const card = document.createElement('div');


    card.classList.add('card');


    card.draggable = true;


    card.textContent = text;





    const cardText = document.createElement('span');


    cardText.classList.add('card-text');


    cardText.textContent = text;





    const delBtn = document.createElement('button');


    delBtn.classList.add('card-del');


    delBtn.textContent = '×';


    delBtn.onclick = function() {


      const container = this.parentNode.parentNode;


      const column = container.parentNode;


      container.removeChild(this.parentNode);


      updateCount(column);


      saveBoard();


    };





    card.appendChild(cardText);


    card.appendChild(delBtn);





    card.addEventListener('dragstart', function(e) {


      e.dataTransfer.setData('text/plain', this.textContent);


      this.classList.add('dragging');


    });





    card.addEventListener('dragend', function() {


      this.classList.remove('dragging');


    });





    return card;


  }





  // Update the count display for a column


  function updateCount(column) {


    const countEl = column.querySelector('.count');


    const count = column.querySelectorAll('.card').length;


    countEl.textContent = count;


  }





  // Save current board state to localStorage


  function saveBoard() {


    const cols = document.querySelec

document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', () => {


    const COL_IDS = ['col-todo','col-doing','col-done'];


    const STORAGE_KEY = 'kanbanBoard';





    /*** UTILITIES ***/


    function uid(){


        return 'c'+Date.now()+Math.random().toString(36).substr(2,5);


    }





    function createCardElement(id, title){


        const card = document.createElement('div');


        card.className = 'card';


        card.draggable = true;


        card.id = id;





        const txt = document.createElement('span');


        txt.textContent = title;


        const delBtn = document.createElement('button');


        delBtn.className = 'card-del';


        delBtn.innerHTML = '&times;';


        delBtn.title = 'Delete';





        delBtn.addEventListener('click', () => {


            card.remove();


            updateCounts();


            saveState();


        });





        card.appendChild(txt);


        card.appendChild(delBtn);





        card.addEventListener('dragstart', e => {


            e.dataTransfer.setData('text/plain', card.id);


            e.dataTransfer.effectAllowed = 'move';


        });





        return card;


    }





    function addCard(title, columnId='col-todo', id=null){

        const cardId = id || uid();


        const card = createCardElement(cardId, title);


        const container = document.querySelector(`#${columnId} .card-container`);


        container.appendChild(card);


        updateCounts();


        saveState();


    }





    function updateCounts(){

            const cntEl = document.querySelector(`#${colId} .count`);


            const num = document.querySelectorAll(`#${colId} .card`).length;


            cntEl.textContent = num;


        });


    }





    function saveState(){

            const cards = [];


            const container = document.querySelector(`#${colId} .card-container`);


            container.querySelectorAll('.card').forEach(card=>{


                cards.push({


                    id: card.id,


                    title: card.querySelector('span').textContent


                });


            });


            state[colId] = cards;


        });


        localStorage.setItem(STORAGE_KEY, JSON.stringify(state));


    }

  let state = [];

  function saveState() {


    try {

  function createCardElement(card) {


    const div = document.createElement('div');


    div.className = 'card';


    div.draggable = true;


    div.dataset.id = card.id;





    const span = document.createElement('span');


    span.className = 'card-title';

    btn.className = 'card-del';


    btn.innerHTML = '&times;';


    btn.title = 'Delete card';


    btn.addEventListener('click', (e) => {


      e.stopPropagation();


      state = state.filter(c => c.id !== card.id);


      saveState();


      render();


    });

    div.appendChild(btn);





    div.addEventListener('dragstart', (e) => {


      draggedCardId = card.id;


      e.dataTransfer.effectAllowed = 'move';


      e.dataTransfer.setData('text/plain', card.id);


    });





    div.addEventListener('dragend', () => {


      draggedCardId = null;


      document.querySelectorAll('.column').forEach(c => c.classList.remove('drag-over'));


    });





    return div;


  }

    try {


      const raw = localStorage.getItem(STORAGE_KEY);


      if (!raw) return null;

  function saveState() {


    const cards = [];

      container.querySelectorAll('.card').forEach(cardEl => {


        cards.push({


          id: cardEl.dataset.id,


          title: cardEl.dataset.title,


          status: status


        });


      });


    });


    localStorage.setItem(STORAGE_KEY, JSON.stringify({ cards: cards }));


  }

      const container = col.querySelector('.card-container');


      const count = container.querySelectorAll('.card').length;


      col.querySelector('.count').textContent = count;


    });


  }

(function() {

      const raw = localStorage.getItem(STORAGE_KEY);


      if (raw) {


        const parsed = JSON.parse(raw);


        if (Array.isArray(parsed)) return parsed;


      }


    } catch (e) {}


    return [];


  }





  function save() {


    localStorage.setItem(STORAGE_KEY, JSON.stringify(state));


  }





  function genId() {


    return 'c-' + Date.now() + '-' + Math.random().toString(36).slice(2, 8);


  }





  function updateCounts() {

      countEl.textContent = columns[col].querySelectorAll('.card').length;


    }


  }





  function makeCardEl(card) {

    el.className = 'card';


    el.setAttribute('draggable', 'true');


    el.dataset.id = card.id;





    const title = document.createElement('span');

    del.className = 'card-del';


    del.textContent = '×';


    del.setAttribute('aria-label', 'Delete card');


    del.addEventListener('click', function(e) {


      e.stopPropagation();


      state = state.filter(c => c.id !== card.id);


      el.remove();


      save();


      updateCounts();


    });





    el.appendChild(title);


    el.appendChild(del);

<script>


const board = document.getElementById('board');


const colTodo = document.getElementById('col-todo');


const colDoing = document.getElementById('col-doing');


const colDone = document.getElementById('col-done');


const newCardInput = document.getElementById('new-card');


const addCardButton = document.getElementById('add-card');





let cards = []; // Store card data: {title, columnId}





function updateCounts() {


  document.querySelectorAll('.count').forEach(count => {


    count.textContent = cards.filter(card => card.columnId === parseInt(count.parentElement.id.slice(1))).length;


  });


}





function renderCards() {


  const colTodoContent = document.getElementById('col-todo-content');


  const colDoingContent = document.getElementById('col-doing-content');


  const colDoneContent = document.getElementById('col-done-content');





  colTodoContent.innerHTML = '';


  colDoingContent.innerHTML = '';


  colDoneContent.innerHTML = '';





  cards.forEach(card => {


    const cardElement = document.createElement('div');


    cardElement.classList.add('card');


    cardElement.textContent = card.title;





    const deleteButton = document.createElement('button');


    deleteButton.classList.add('card-del');


    deleteButton.textContent = 'Delete';





    cardElement.appendChild(deleteButton);





    if (parseInt(card.columnId) === 1) {


      colTodoContent.appendChild(cardElement);


    } else if (parseInt(card.columnId) === 2) {


      colDoingContent.appendChild(cardElement);


    } else {


      colDoneContent.appendChild(cardElement);


    }


  });





  updateCounts();


}








function addCard(title) {


  if (title.trim() !== '') {


    const newColumnId = parseInt(document.getElementById('col-todo').id.slice(1)); // Get column id


    cards.push({ title: title, columnId: newColumnId });


    renderCards();


    newCardInput.value = '';


  }


}





function dropCard(target) {


  const card = target.closest('.card');


  if (card) {


    const originalColumnId = parseInt(card.dataset.columnId);


    const newColumnId = parseInt(target.id.slice(1));





    // Remove from old column and add to new column


    cards = cards.filter(c => c.columnId !== originalColumnId);


    cards.push({ title: card.textContent, columnId: newColumnId });


    renderCards();


  }


}








const colTodoElement = colTodo;


const colDoingElement = colDoing;


const colDoneElement = colDone;





colTodoElement.addEventListener('dragstart', (e) => {


  e.dataTransfer.setData('text/plain', e.target.dataset.columnId);


});





colTodoElement.addEventListener('dragover', (e) => {


  e.preventDefault();


});





colTodoElement.addEventListener('drop', (e) => {


    dropCard(e.target);


});








colDoingElement.addEventListener('dragover', (e) => {


  e.preventDefault();


});





colDoingElement.addEventListener('drop', (e) => {


    dropCard(e.target);


});





colDoneElement.addEventListener('dragover', (e) => {


  e.preventDefault();


});





colDoneElement.addEventListener('drop', (e) => {


    dropCard(e.target);


});








addCardButton.addEventListener('click', () => {


  const title = newCardInput.value;


  addCard(title);


});








// Load from localStorage on page load


if (localStorage.getItem('cards')) {


  cards = JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem('cards'));


  renderCards();


}





// Save to localStorage whenever cards change


cards.forEach(card => {


    const colElement = document.getElementById(card.columnId);


    if(colElement){


        const cardElement = document.createElement("div");


        cardElement.classList.add("card");


        cardElement.textContent = card.title;


        cardElement.dataset.columnId = card.columnId; // Store column id





        const deleteButton = document.createElement('button');


        deleteButton.classList.add('card-del');


        deleteButton.textContent = 'Delete';





        cardElement.appendChild(deleteButton);


        if (parseInt(card.columnId) === 1){


            colTodo.appendChild(cardElement);

Claude has no cost figure here, on purpose. Every Claude model is measured through the Claude Code CLI, which runs on a subscription — there is no per-token price to report, so any number would be invented. Every other row is what the provider billed, or list pricing on real token counts. We tried publishing a derived API-equivalent and measurement refuted it twice: the CLI sends its own instructions, reads its own files and carries its own conversation, so it consumed 16× more input per turn than the same model doing the same task through an API — a different agent, not a fixed overhead, and nothing you can subtract. Claude cost lands here when the full suite has been run through the API, not before.
Latest-per-model table ranks by score, then cost, then speed. Token counts come from provider usage fields. Outputs are verbatim transcripts, truncated for display — full text lives in runs/…/transcript.jsonl.
